Multiplicity-free representations of the principal A 1 \mathchar 28993_{\mathchar 28721} -subgroup in a simple algebraic group
Aluna Rizzoli, Donna M. Testerman

Abstract
Let G \mathchar 28999 be a simple algebraic group defined over an algebraically closed field 𝓀 \mathcal{\mathchar 29035} of characteristic p > 0 \mathchar 29040\mathchar 12606\mathchar 28720 . Here we classify all irreducible 𝓀 ​ G \mathcal{\mathchar 29035}\mathchar 28999 -modules for which the principal A 1 \mathchar 28993_{\mathchar 28721} has no repeated composition factors, extending the work of Liebeck-Seitz-Testerman which treated the same question when 𝓀 \mathcal{\mathchar 29035} is replaced by an algebraically closed field of characteristic zero.



1
Introduction

In this paper, we consider a question in the representation theory and subgroup structure of simple algebraic groups defined over an algebraically closed
field
\mathcal{\mathchar 29035}
of characteristic
\mathchar 29040\mathchar 12606\mathchar 28720
. The main aim of our work is to generalise the results of
[
12
]
,
[
14
]
, and
[
13
]
, where the authors consider so-called “multiplicity-free
subgroups” of simple algebraic groups defined over an
algebraically closed field
\mathchar 29003
of characteristic 0. More precisely,
the authors consider triples
\delimiter 67273472\mathchar 29016\mathchar 24891\mathchar 29017\mathchar 24891\mathchar 29014\delimiter 84054785
where
\mathchar 29016
and
\mathchar 29017
are simple algebraic groups defined over
\mathchar 29003
with
\mathchar 29016
a closed subgroup of
\mathchar 29017
, and
\mathchar 29014
is an
irreducible
\mathchar 29003\mathchar 29017
-module such that
\mathchar 29014\delimiter 52573049\mathchar 29016
is a sum of non-isomorphic irreducible

\mathchar 29003\mathchar 29016
-modules (a so-called “multiplicity-free”
\mathchar 29003\mathchar 29016
-module). The above cited articles provide a complete classification of such triples when either
\mathchar 29016

has rank 1 and does not lie in a proper parabolic subgroup of
\mathchar 29017
, or
\mathchar 29017
is a classical group with natural module
\mathchar 29015
and
\mathchar 29016
is of type
\mathchar 28993_{\mathchar 352}
acting
irreducibly on
\mathchar 29015
. Note that the case where
\mathchar 29016
acts irreducibly (and hence multiplicity freely) on
\mathchar 29014
was settled in work of
Dynkin
[
6
]
, in characteristic zero, and in work of Seitz and Testerman in positive characteristic
[
18
,
21
]
.

The ultimate far-reaching aim of what we undertake in this paper would be to investigate the “multiplicity-free” triples
\delimiter 67273472\mathchar 29016\mathchar 24891\mathchar 29017\mathchar 24891\mathchar 29014\delimiter 84054785
as in
[
12
]
,
[
14
]
, and
[
13
]
, described above, replacing the field
\mathchar 29003
by the field of positive characteristic
\mathcal{\mathchar 29035}
, and considering composition factors rather than summands. The proofs in
[
14
,
13
]
use induction on the rank of the group
\mathchar 29016
; the case where
\mathchar 29016
is simple of rank 1 is considered in
[
12
]
.
Here we treat the rank one case for the groups defined over
\mathcal{\mathchar 29035}
, but consider a slightly more general setting than would strictly speaking be required for use in an inductive set-up. Namely, we consider all
simple algebraic groups
\mathchar 28999
(classical and exceptional), defined over
\mathcal{\mathchar 29035}
, and
\mathchar 28993
a closed
\mathchar 28993_{\mathchar 28721}
-type subgroup of
\mathchar 28999
containing a regular unipotent element of
\mathchar 28999
, which
we will call
a “principal

\mathchar 28993_{\mathchar 28721}
-subgroup” of
\mathchar 28999
. (There is at most one conjugacy class of such subgroups.) We then determine
all irreducible
\mathcal{\mathchar 29035}\mathchar 28999
-modules
\mathchar 29014
such that
\mathchar 29014\delimiter 52573049\mathchar 28993
has non-isomorphic composition factors, and obtain a classification analogous to

[
12
, Thm. 1]
. Much of the analysis follows the same line of reasoning as that used in
[
12
]
; the main differences and difficulties arise from the lack of
precise knowledge about the dimensions of irreducible
\mathcal{\mathchar 29035}\mathchar 28999
-modules and the multiplicities of their weights. In addition, while irreducible

\mathcal{\mathchar 29035}\mathchar 28993_{\mathchar 28721}
-modules are completely understood, the description of the set of weights is not as simple as in characteristic zero. In
[
14
,
13
]
, another essential ingredient of the proof is the work of Stembridge
[
20
]
, where he determines when the tensor product of two irreducible modules for a simple algebraic group defined over the field
\mathchar 29003
has no repeated irreducible summands. There has been recent progress on the analogous question for the simple groups defined over fields of positive characteristic in
[
7
]
. The combination of the rank-
\mathchar 28721
theorem proven here and Gruber’s work lays the foundation for the study of multiplicity-free subgroups of higher rank for the groups defined over fields of positive characteristic.

In order to state our main result, we introduce some notation; further notation will be set up in §

\mathchar 28999
a simply connected simple algebraic

\mathchar 28999

\mathchar 28994

with
\mathchar 29012\mathchar 12826\mathchar 28994

\mathchar 29012

Theorem 1
.

Suppose that
\mathchar 28949
is
\mathchar 29040
-restricted. Then
\mathchar 29004\delimiter 67273472\mathchar 28949\delimiter 84054785\delimiter 52573049\mathchar 28993
is
\mathchar 29005\mathchar 28998
if and only if one of the following holds.

(i)

We have that
\mathchar 29040\mathchar 12606\delimiter 67273472\mathchar 28949\delimiter 52573049\mathchar 29012_{\mathchar 28993}\delimiter 84054785
and
\mathchar 28673_{\mathchar 29003}\delimiter 67273472\mathchar 28949\delimiter 84054785\delimiter 52573049\mathchar 28993_{\mathchar 29003}
is MF, or

(ii)

the group
\mathchar 28999
is of type
\mathchar 28993_{\mathchar 28722}
,
\mathchar 28949\mathchar 12349\mathchar 28961_{\mathchar 28721}\mathchar 8235\mathchar 28961_{\mathchar 28722}
and
\mathchar 29040\mathchar 12349\mathchar 28723
, or

(iii)

the group
\mathchar 28999
is of type
\mathchar 28994_{\mathchar 28722}
,
\mathchar 28949\mathchar 12349\mathchar 28722\mathchar 28961_{\mathchar 28721}
and
\mathchar 29040\mathchar 12349\mathchar 28725
.

Corollary 2
.

\mathchar 28949\mathchar 12349\mathchar 4944\displaylimits_{\mathchar 29033\mathchar 12349\mathchar 28720}^{\mathchar 29044}\mathchar 29040^{\mathchar 29033}\mathchar 28949_{\mathchar 29033}

We conclude the introduction with a few remarks about the proof. We first note that if
\mathchar 29040\mathchar 12606\mathchar 29042
, then one can show that the Weyl module with highest weight

\mathchar 28949
is an irreducible
\mathcal{\mathchar 29035}\mathchar 28999
-module (see
[
11
, Cor.2.7.6]
), and then the considerations of
[
12
]
for the groups defined over
\mathchar 29003
yield the result. (See Proposition
2.2
)
The arguments therefore focus on the cases where
\mathchar 29040\mathchar 12820\mathchar 29042
.
Many aspects of the proof follow closely the arguments used in
[
12
]
. In particular, we use the fact
that all irreducible
\mathcal{\mathchar 29035}\mathchar 28993_{\mathchar 28721}
-modules have multiplicity one weight spaces and therefore considering the set of
\mathchar 29012_{\mathchar 28993}
-weights and their multiplicities in
\mathchar 29014
can directly be used
to detect multiplicities of composition factors of
\mathchar 29014\delimiter 52573049\mathchar 28993
. Moreover, there are certain dimension bounds which must be respected by an MF-module. Thus, many of our preliminary lemmas are inspired by the
results in

[
12
, Section 2]
. In addition, we rely on a result from
[
8
]
showing that the principal
\mathchar 28993_{\mathchar 28721}
-subgroup
\mathchar 28993
of
\mathchar 28999
satisfies the property that certain tilting modules for

\mathchar 28999
have a filtration by tilting modules for
\mathchar 28993
. Since indecomposable reducible tilting modules for
\mathchar 28993_{\mathchar 28721}
necessarily have repeated composition factors, this result is quite useful for
showing that many
\mathcal{\mathchar 29035}\mathchar 28999
-modules are not MF as
\mathcal{\mathchar 29035}\mathchar 28993
-modules.

2
Preliminary lemmas

Let us fix the notation to be used throughout the paper, in addition to that which was introduced in the previous section.

We assume throughout that
\mathchar 352\mathchar 12821\mathchar 28722
, respectively
\mathchar 28722\mathchar 24891\mathchar 28723\mathchar 24891\mathchar 28724
, for
\mathchar 28999
of type

, respectively

\mathchar 28939_{\mathchar 29033}
. For

, a dominant weight, we write
\mathchar 28673_{\mathchar 28999}\delimiter 67273472\mathchar 28949\delimiter 84054785
for the Weyl module for
\mathchar 28999
of highest weight
\mathchar 28949
. We will suppress the
\mathchar 28999
in this notation if there is no ambiguity. For a
\mathcal{\mathchar 29035}\mathchar 28999
-module
\mathchar 29014
and
\mathchar 28950\mathchar 12850\mathchar 29016\delimiter 67273472\mathchar 29012\delimiter 84054785

-weight space with respect to
\mathchar 29012
of the module
\mathchar 29014

-module of highest weight

\mathchar 28993_{\mathchar 28721}
-subgroup
\mathchar 28993

Lemma 2.1
.

If
\mathchar 29040\mathchar 12606\mathchar 29042
, then
\mathchar 28673\delimiter 67273472\mathchar 28949\delimiter 84054785
is irreducible.

Proof.

, Part II, 8.19]
, it suffices to prove that for all
\mathchar 28939\mathchar 12850\mathchar 28680^{\mathchar 8235}
, we have
\mathchar 29042\mathchar 12821\delimiter 69632778\mathchar 28949\mathchar 8235\mathchar 28942\mathchar 24891\mathchar 28939\delimiter 86414091\mathchar 8704\mathchar 28721
, where
\mathchar 28942\mathchar 12349\mathchar 4944\displaylimits_{\mathchar 29033\mathchar 12349\mathchar 28721}^{\mathchar 352}\mathchar 28961_{\mathchar 29033}
. It is easy to see that
\delimiter 69632778\mathchar 28949\mathchar 24891\mathchar 28939\delimiter 86414091

is the highest root of the dual root system
\mathchar 28680^{\mathchar 8799}
, i.e. when
\mathchar 28939
is the highest short root
\mathchar 28940
of
\mathchar 28680
. By
[

, we have
\delimiter 69632778\mathchar 28949\mathchar 8235\mathchar 28942\mathchar 24891\mathchar 28940\delimiter 86414091\mathchar 12820\mathchar 28721\mathchar 8235\mathchar 4944\displaylimits_{\mathchar 28939\mathchar 12850\mathchar 28680^{\mathchar 8235}}\delimiter 69632778\mathchar 28949\mathchar 24891\mathchar 28939\delimiter 86414091
. It is therefore sufficient to show that
\mathchar 29042\mathchar 12349\mathchar 4944\displaylimits_{\mathchar 28939\mathchar 12850\mathchar 28680^{\mathchar 8235}}\delimiter 69632778\mathchar 28949\mathchar 24891\mathchar 28939\delimiter 86414091
, which is a simple calculation using the fact that for a simple root
\mathchar 28939_{\mathchar 29033}
we have
\mathchar 4944\displaylimits_{\mathchar 28939\mathchar 12850\mathchar 28680^{\mathchar 8235}\mathchar 8814\{\mathchar 28939_{\mathchar 29033}\}}\delimiter 69632778\mathchar 28939_{\mathchar 29033}\mathchar 24891\mathchar 28939\delimiter 86414091\mathchar 12349\mathchar 4944\displaylimits_{\mathchar 28939\mathchar 12850\mathchar 28680^{\mathchar 8235}\mathchar 8814\{\mathchar 28939_{\mathchar 29033}\}}\delimiter 69632778\mathchar 29043_{\mathchar 29033}\delimiter 67273472\mathchar 28939_{\mathchar 29033}\delimiter 84054785\mathchar 24891\mathchar 29043_{\mathchar 29033}\delimiter 67273472\mathchar 28939\delimiter 84054785\delimiter 86414091\mathchar 12349\mathchar 4944\displaylimits_{\mathchar 28939\mathchar 12850\mathchar 28680^{\mathchar 8235}\mathchar 8814\{\mathchar 28939_{\mathchar 29033}\}}\delimiter 69632778\mathchar 8704\mathchar 28939_{\mathchar 29033}\mathchar 24891\mathchar 28939\delimiter 86414091

Proposition 2.2
.

Assume
\mathchar 29040\mathchar 12606\mathchar 29042
. Then
\mathchar 28673_{\mathchar 29003}\delimiter 67273472\mathchar 28949\delimiter 84054785\delimiter 52573049\mathchar 28993_{\mathchar 29003}

\mathchar 29014\delimiter 52573049\mathchar 28993

Proof.

By Lemma
2.1
, the Weyl module is irreducible and therefore
\mathchar 29014\mathchar 12349\mathchar 28673\delimiter 67273472\mathchar 28949\delimiter 84054785
. We have
\mathchar 28673_{\mathchar 29003}\delimiter 67273472\mathchar 28949\delimiter 84054785\delimiter 52573049\mathchar 28993_{\mathchar 29003}\mathchar 12349\mathchar 4944\displaylimits_{\mathchar 28720}^{\mathchar 29035}\mathchar 28673_{\mathchar 28993_{\mathchar 29003}}\delimiter 67273472\mathchar 29042_{\mathchar 29033}\delimiter 84054785
for some integers
\mathchar 29042_{\mathchar 28720}\mathchar 12821\mathchar 29042_{\mathchar 28721}\dots\mathchar 12821\mathchar 29042_{\mathchar 29035}\mathchar 12821\mathchar 28720
with
\mathchar 29042_{\mathchar 28720}\mathchar 12349\mathchar 29042
. Since
\mathchar 29040\mathchar 12606\mathchar 29042
, a comparison of characters gives
\mathchar 28673\delimiter 67273472\mathchar 28949\delimiter 84054785\delimiter 52573049\mathchar 28993\mathchar 12349\mathchar 4944\displaylimits_{\mathchar 28720}^{\mathchar 29035}\mathchar 28673_{\mathchar 28993}\delimiter 67273472\mathchar 29042_{\mathchar 29033}\delimiter 84054785\mathchar 12349\mathchar 4944\displaylimits_{\mathchar 28720}^{\mathchar 29035}\delimiter 67273472\mathchar 29042_{\mathchar 29033}\delimiter 84054785
, which then implies the result.
∎

As the next result shows, in many cases when
\mathchar 28673\delimiter 67273472\mathchar 28949\delimiter 84054785
is irreducible and
\mathchar 29042\mathchar 12821\mathchar 29040
, we can directly conclude that
\mathchar 29014\delimiter 52573049\mathchar 28993
is not MF.

Lemma 2.3
.

Assume that
\mathchar 28673\delimiter 67273472\mathchar 28949\delimiter 84054785
is irreducible and if
\mathchar 28999
is of type
\mathchar 28994_{\mathchar 352}
, respectively
\mathchar 28996_{\mathchar 352}
, and
\mathchar 28949
does not lie in the root lattice of

\mathchar 28999
, assume in addition that
\mathchar 29040\mathchar 12606{{\mathchar 352\mathchar 8235\mathchar 28721}\choose{\mathchar 28722}}
, respectively
\mathchar 29040\mathchar 12606{\mathchar 352\choose\mathchar 28722}
. If
\mathchar 29042\mathchar 12821\mathchar 29040
and
\mathchar 29042\mathrel{{\mathchar 566\mathchar 529}}\mathchar 8704\mathchar 28721\mod\mathchar 29040
, then
\mathchar 29014\delimiter 52573049\mathchar 28993
is not MF.

Proof.

Here we use
[
8
, Theorems 4.1.2, 4.1.4 and 4.2.1]
to see that
\mathchar 28993
is a so-called “good filtration” subgroup, which then implies that the irreducible Weyl module
\mathchar 28673\delimiter 67273472\mathchar 28949\delimiter 84054785\mathchar 12349\mathchar 29014

has an
\mathchar 28993
-filtration by
both Weyl modules and by induced modules. So in particular,
\mathchar 29014\delimiter 52573049\mathchar 28993
is a tilting module. Since tilting modules for
\mathchar 28993
are self-dual, no reducible indecomposable
tilting module is MF. The hypotheses on
\mathchar 29042
imply that the indecomposable tilting module
\mathchar 29012\delimiter 67273472\mathchar 29042\delimiter 84054785
is reducible (see
[
9
, Theorem 2.1]
). As
\mathchar 29012\delimiter 67273472\mathchar 29042\delimiter 84054785
is a summand of
\mathchar 29014\delimiter 52573049\mathchar 28993
, this concludes the proof.
∎

Lemma 2.7

(2)

..

(for example), one sees that
\mathchar 28673\delimiter 67273472\mathchar 28949\delimiter 84054785

. If

\mathchar 29025\mathchar 8235\mathchar 29026\mathchar 12349\mathchar 29040\mathchar 8704\mathchar 28721
, and we conclude by Lemma

.
∎

Lemma 3.19
.

Assume
\mathchar 29025\mathchar 12821\mathchar 28722
,
\mathchar 29026\mathchar 12821\mathchar 28721
and set
\mathchar 28951\mathchar 12349\mathchar 28949\mathchar 8704\mathchar 28722\mathchar 28721
. The following hold.

(i)

If
\mathchar 29025\mathchar 8235\mathchar 28723\mathchar 29026\mathchar 8235\mathchar 28723\mathrel{{\mathchar 566\mathchar 529}}\mathchar 28720\mod\mathchar 29040
and
\mathchar 28722\mathchar 29025\mathchar 8235\mathchar 28723\mathchar 29026\mathchar 8235\mathchar 28724\mathrel{{\mathchar 566\mathchar 529}}\mathchar 28720\mod\mathchar 29040
, then
\dim\mathchar 29014_{\mathchar 28951}\mathchar 12349\mathchar 28723
.

(ii)

If
\mathchar 29025\mathchar 8235\mathchar 28723\mathchar 29026\mathchar 8235\mathchar 28723\mathchar 12817\mathchar 28720\mod\mathchar 29040
, then
\dim\mathchar 29014_{\mathchar 28951}\mathchar 12349\mathchar 28722
.

(iii)

If
\mathchar 28722\mathchar 29025\mathchar 8235\mathchar 28723\mathchar 29026\mathchar 8235\mathchar 28724\mathchar 12817\mathchar 28720\mod\mathchar 29040
, then
\dim\mathchar 29014_{\mathchar 28951}\mathchar 12349\mathchar 28722
.

Proof..

Using
[
16
]
and
[

, Proposition A]
, one checks that
\dim\mathchar 28673\delimiter 67273472\mathchar 28949\delimiter 84054785_{\mathchar 28951}\mathchar 12349\mathchar 28723
. Note as well that if
\mathchar 28945
is a dominant weight satisfying
\mathchar 28951\mathchar 12830\mathchar 28945\mathchar 12830\mathchar 28949

\mathchar 28945\mathchar 12850\{\mathchar 28949\mathchar 8704\mathchar 28721\mathchar 28720\mathchar 24891\mathchar 28949\mathchar 8704\mathchar 28720\mathchar 28721\ \delimiter 67273472{\text{if }}\mathchar 29026\mathchar 12821\mathchar 28722\delimiter 84054785\mathchar 24891\mathchar 28949\mathchar 8704\mathchar 28722\mathchar 28720\ \delimiter 67273472{\text{if }}\mathchar 29025\mathchar 12821\mathchar 28724\delimiter 84054785\mathchar 24891\mathchar 28950\}

\mathchar 28945

First consider the case where

(see Lemma

and
\mathchar 29030_{\mathchar 28939_{\mathchar 28722}}\mathchar 29030_{\mathchar 28939_{\mathchar 28721}}\delimiter 69640972^{\mathchar 8235}
are linearly independent, where
\delimiter 69640972^{\mathchar 8235}
is a highest weight vector.
The weight space
\mathchar 29014_{\mathchar 28951}
is spanned by the vectors
\delimiter 69640972_{\mathchar 28721}\mathchar 12349\mathchar 29030_{\mathchar 28722\mathchar 28939_{\mathchar 28721}\mathchar 8235\mathchar 28939_{\mathchar 28722}}\delimiter 69640972^{\mathchar 8235}

. Suppose

, for
\mathchar 29025_{\mathchar 29033}\mathchar 12850\mathcal{\mathchar 29035}
. Then applying
\mathchar 29029_{\mathchar 28939_{\mathchar 28721}}
and
\mathchar 29029_{\mathchar 28939_{\mathchar 28722}}
respectively, and using the fact that
\mathchar 29030_{\mathchar 28939_{\mathchar 28721}}^{\mathchar 28722}\delimiter 69640972^{\mathchar 8235}\mathrel{{{{\mathchar 566\mathchar 61}}}}\mathchar 28720
, we obtain the following system of equations :

\mathchar 28722\mathchar 29025_{\mathchar 28721}\mathchar 8235\mathchar 29025\mathchar 29025_{\mathchar 28722}\mathchar 12349\mathchar 28720\mathchar 24891\quad\mathchar 28723\mathchar 29025_{\mathchar 28722}\mathchar 8235\mathchar 29025_{\mathchar 28723}\delimiter 67273472\mathchar 28722\mathchar 29025\mathchar 8704\mathchar 28722\delimiter 84054785\mathchar 12349\mathchar 28720\mbox{ and }\mathchar 29025_{\mathchar 28723}\delimiter 67273472\mathchar 29026\mathchar 8235\mathchar 28722\delimiter 84054785\mathchar 8704\mathchar 29025_{\mathchar 28722}\mathchar 12349\mathchar 28720\mathchar 314

\delimiter 69640972_{\mathchar 28721}\mathchar 24891\delimiter 69640972_{\mathchar 28722}\mathchar 24891\delimiter 69640972_{\mathchar 28723}

Lemma 3.20
.

Let
\mathchar 29025\mathchar 12349\mathchar 28721
,
\mathchar 29026\mathchar 12821\mathchar 28721
, and set
\mathchar 28951\mathchar 12349\mathchar 28949\mathchar 8704\mathchar 28722\mathchar 28721
. Then
\dim\mathchar 29014_{\mathchar 28951}\mathchar 12349\mathchar 28721
if
\mathchar 28723\mathchar 29026\mathchar 8235\mathchar 28724\mathchar 12817\mathchar 28720\mod\mathchar 29040
and
\dim\mathchar 29014_{\mathchar 28951}\mathchar 12349\mathchar 28722
otherwise.

Proof..

Using
[
16
]
and
[
5
, Proposition A]
, we have
\dim\mathchar 28673\delimiter 67273472\mathchar 28949\delimiter 84054785_{\mathchar 28951}\mathchar 12349\mathchar 28722
and
\mathchar 29014_{\mathchar 28951}
is spanned by
\delimiter 69640972_{\mathchar 28721}\mathchar 12349\mathchar 29030_{\mathchar 28722\mathchar 28939_{\mathchar 28721}\mathchar 8235\mathchar 28939_{\mathchar 28722}}\delimiter 69640972^{\mathchar 8235}
and
\delimiter 69640972_{\mathchar 28722}\mathchar 12349\mathchar 29030_{\mathchar 28939_{\mathchar 28721}\mathchar 8235\mathchar 28939_{\mathchar 28722}}\mathchar 29030_{\mathchar 28939_{\mathchar 28721}}\delimiter 69640972^{\mathchar 8235}
. If
\mathchar 28723\mathchar 29026\mathchar 8235\mathchar 28724\mathchar 12817\mathchar 28720\mod\mathchar 29040
, then by Lemma
2.16
,
\mathchar 28950
affords a composition factor of
\mathchar 28673\delimiter 67273472\mathchar 28949\delimiter 84054785
and
\mathchar 28951
occurs with multiplicity 1 there. So by
[
17
]
, we have
\dim\mathchar 29014_{\mathchar 28951}\mathchar 12349\mathchar 28721
.

If
\mathchar 28723\mathchar 29026\mathchar 8235\mathchar 28724\mathrel{{\mathchar 566\mathchar 529}}\mathchar 28720\mod\mathchar 29040
, then
\mathchar 28950
does not afford a composition factor and
\mathchar 29030_{\mathchar 28939_{\mathchar 28721}\mathchar 8235\mathchar 28939_{\mathchar 28722}}\delimiter 69640972^{\mathchar 8235}
and
\mathchar 29030_{\mathchar 28939_{\mathchar 28722}}\mathchar 29030_{\mathchar 28939_{\mathchar 28721}}\delimiter 69640972^{\mathchar 8235}
are linearly independent. If
\mathchar 29025_{\mathchar 28721}\delimiter 69640972_{\mathchar 28721}\mathchar 8235\mathchar 29025_{\mathchar 28722}\delimiter 69640972_{\mathchar 28722}\mathchar 12349\mathchar 28720
for

\mathchar 29029_{\mathchar 28939_{\mathchar 28721}}

\mathchar 29029_{\mathchar 28939_{\mathchar 28722}}

. Hence the two vectors are linearly independent and
\dim\mathchar 29014_{\mathchar 28951}\mathchar 12349\mathchar 28722
.∎

Lemma 3.21
.

Assume
\mathchar 29026\mathchar 12821\mathchar 28722
,
\mathchar 29025\mathchar 12821\mathchar 28721
, and set
\mathchar 28951\mathchar 12349\mathchar 28949\mathchar 8704\mathchar 28721\mathchar 28722
. Then
\dim\mathchar 29014_{\mathchar 28951}\mathchar 12349\mathchar 28721
if
\mathchar 29025\mathchar 8235\mathchar 28723\mathchar 29026\mathchar 8235\mathchar 28723\mathchar 12817\mathchar 28720\mod\mathchar 29040
and
\dim\mathchar 29014_{\mathchar 28951}\mathchar 12349\mathchar 28722
otherwise.

Proof..

As in the preceding lemmas, we find that
\dim\mathchar 28673\delimiter 67273472\mathchar 28949\delimiter 84054785_{\mathchar 28951}\mathchar 12349\mathchar 28722
. If
\mathchar 29025\mathchar 8235\mathchar 28723\mathchar 29026\mathchar 8235\mathchar 28723\mathchar 12817\mathchar 28720\mod\mathchar 29040
, then
\mathchar 28950
affords a composition factor of
\mathchar 28673\delimiter 67273472\mathchar 28949\delimiter 84054785
and using
[
17
]
we deduce that
\dim\mathchar 29014_{\mathchar 28951}\mathchar 12349\mathchar 28721
.

So assume that
\mathchar 29025\mathchar 8235\mathchar 28723\mathchar 29026\mathchar 8235\mathchar 28723\mathrel{{\mathchar 566\mathchar 529}}\mathchar 28720\mod\mathchar 29040
and then
\mathchar 29030_{\mathchar 28939_{\mathchar 28721}\mathchar 8235\mathchar 28939_{\mathchar 28722}}\delimiter 69640972^{\mathchar 8235}
and
\mathchar 29030_{\mathchar 28939_{\mathchar 28722}}\mathchar 29030_{\mathchar 28939_{\mathchar 28721}}\delimiter 69640972^{\mathchar 8235}
are linearly independent. The
\mathchar 28951
weight space is spanned by
\delimiter 69640972_{\mathchar 28721}\mathchar 12349\mathchar 29030_{\mathchar 28939_{\mathchar 28721}\mathchar 8235\mathchar 28939_{\mathchar 28722}}\mathchar 29030_{\mathchar 28939_{\mathchar 28722}}\delimiter 69640972^{\mathchar 8235}
and
\delimiter 69640972_{\mathchar 28722}\mathchar 12349\mathchar 29030_{\mathchar 28939_{\mathchar 28722}}^{\mathchar 28722}\mathchar 29030_{\mathchar 28939_{\mathchar 28721}}\delimiter 69640972^{\mathchar 8235}
. Suppose
\mathchar 29025_{\mathchar 28721}\delimiter 69640972_{\mathchar 28721}\mathchar 8235\mathchar 29025_{\mathchar 28722}\delimiter 69640972_{\mathchar 28722}\mathchar 12349\mathchar 28720
for
\mathchar 29025_{\mathchar 29033}\mathchar 12850\mathcal{\mathchar 29035}
. Applying

\mathchar 29029_{\mathchar 28939_{\mathchar 28721}}
and
\mathchar 29029_{\mathchar 28939_{\mathchar 28722}}
and using that
\mathchar 29030_{\mathchar 28939_{\mathchar 28722}}^{\mathchar 28722}\delimiter 69640972^{\mathchar 8235}\mathrel{{{{\mathchar 566\mathchar 61}}}}\mathchar 28720
, we deduce that
\mathchar 28723\mathchar 29025_{\mathchar 28721}\mathchar 8235\mathchar 29025\mathchar 29025_{\mathchar 28722}\mathchar 12349\mathchar 28720
and
\mathchar 29025_{\mathchar 28721}\mathchar 29026\mathchar 12349\mathchar 28720
. Hence the two vectors are linearly independent, giving the result. ∎

We are now ready to prove the main proposition.

\mathchar 28723

Proposition 4.7
.

Suppose that
\mathchar 28999
has rank at least
\mathchar 28723
and
\mathchar 28949\mathchar 12349\mathchar 29027_{\mathchar 29033}\mathchar 28961_{\mathchar 29033}\mathchar 8235\mathchar 29027_{\mathchar 29034}\mathchar 28961_{\mathchar 29034}

Lemma 4.15
.

Assume
\mathchar 28999\mathchar 12349\mathchar 28997_{\mathchar 352}
or
\mathchar 28998_{\mathchar 28724}
and
\mathchar 28949\mathchar 12349\mathchar 28961_{\mathchar 29033}

Proof..

\mathchar 29012_{\mathchar 28993}
-weight

\mathchar 28949\mathchar 8704\mathchar 28723\mathchar 28724

\mathchar 28949\mathchar 8704\mathchar 28722\mathchar 28724

\mathchar 28949\mathchar 8704\mathchar 28724\mathchar 28725
. Therefore
\mathchar 29038_{\mathchar 28722}\mathchar 12821\mathchar 28723
, and by Lemma
2.8
(iii), the module
\mathchar 29014\delimiter 52573049\mathchar 28993
is not MF.

If

\mathchar 28949\mathchar 12349\mathchar 28961_{\mathchar 28723}
or
\mathchar 28961_{\mathchar 28726}

\mathchar 29042\mathchar 12349\mathchar 28721\mathchar 28728\mathchar 28722
respectively
\mathchar 29042\mathchar 12349\mathchar 28721\mathchar 28726\mathchar 28728

\mathchar 28994_{\mathchar 29003}\delimiter 67273472\mathchar 29042\delimiter 84054785\mathchar 12349\mathchar 28728\mathchar 28724\mathchar 28726\mathchar 28724
and
\mathchar 28727\mathchar 28722\mathchar 28722\mathchar 28725

\dim\mathchar 29014\mathchar 12606\mathchar 28994_{\mathchar 29003}\delimiter 67273472\mathchar 29042\delimiter 84054785

In all remaining cases, by

]

\mathchar 29014\mathchar 12349\mathchar 28673\delimiter 67273472\mathchar 28949\delimiter 84054785

∎

Proposition 4.16
.

Suppose that
\mathchar 28999
has rank at least
\mathchar 28723
and
\mathchar 28949\mathchar 12349\mathchar 29026\mathchar 28961_{\mathchar 29033}

4.14
,
4.15

∎

5

Let us restate, and prove, Corollary

.

Corollary 5.2
.

\mathchar 28949\mathchar 12349\mathchar 4944\displaylimits_{\mathchar 29033\mathchar 12349\mathchar 28720}^{\mathchar 29044}\mathchar 29040^{\mathchar 29033}\mathchar 28949_{\mathchar 29033}

\mathchar 28720\mathchar 12820\mathchar 29033\mathchar 12820\mathchar 29044

and

\mathchar 28949_{\mathchar 29033}\mathchar 12349\mathchar 28961_{\mathchar 28721}\mathchar 8235\mathchar 28961_{\mathchar 28722}

\mathchar 28949_{\mathchar 29034}\mathchar 12850\{\mathchar 28720\mathchar 24891\mathchar 28961_{\mathchar 28721}\mathchar 8235\mathchar 28961_{\mathchar 28722}\mathchar 24891\mathchar 28961_{\mathchar 28721}\mathchar 24891\mathchar 28961_{\mathchar 28722}\}

\mathchar 28949_{\mathchar 29034\mathchar 8235\mathchar 28721}\mathchar 12349\mathchar 28720

\mathchar 28999

\mathchar 28994_{\mathchar 28722}
,
\mathchar 29040\mathchar 12349\mathchar 28725

\mathchar 28720\mathchar 12820\mathchar 29033\mathchar 12820\mathchar 29044

\mathchar 28949_{\mathchar 29033}\mathchar 12349\mathchar 28722\mathchar 28961_{\mathchar 28721}

\mathchar 28949_{\mathchar 29034}\mathchar 12850\{\mathchar 28720\mathchar 24891\mathchar 28722\mathchar 28961_{\mathchar 28721}\mathchar 24891\mathchar 28961_{\mathchar 28721}\mathchar 24891\mathchar 28961_{\mathchar 28722}\}

\mathchar 28949_{\mathchar 29034}\mathchar 12349\mathchar 28722\mathchar 28961_{\mathchar 28721}

\mathchar 28949_{\mathchar 29034\mathchar 8235\mathchar 28721}\mathchar 12850\{\mathchar 28720\mathchar 24891\mathchar 28961_{\mathchar 28722}\}

Proof..

Without loss of generality, we may assume that
\mathchar 28949_{\mathchar 28720}\mathrel{{{{\mathchar 566\mathchar 61}}}}\mathchar 28720

\mathchar 29044

\mathchar 29044\mathchar 12349\mathchar 28720

\mathchar 28949
is
\mathchar 29040
-restricted and the statement follows from Theorem
1
. Suppose that the statement is valid for all
\mathchar 28720\mathchar 12820\mathchar 29044\mathchar 12820\mathchar 29006

\mathchar 29006\mathchar 12850\mathbb{\mathchar 29006}

\mathchar 28999\mathchar 12349\mathchar 28993_{\mathchar 28722}

\mathchar 29040\mathchar 12349\mathchar 28723

for
